=== ERD.md ===
# Database ERD

```mermaid
erDiagram
    PROVINCES ||--o{ SUPPLIERS : identifies
    FRUITS ||--o{ PRODUCTS : configured_as
    BOX_SIZES ||--o{ PRODUCTS : sized_as
    FRUITS ||--o{ FRUIT_SOURCE_PROVINCES : sourced_from
    PROVINCES ||--o{ FRUIT_SOURCE_PROVINCES : source_province
    SUPPLIERS ||--o{ PURCHASES : supplies
    PRODUCTS ||--o{ PURCHASES : received_as
    STREET_VENDORS ||--o{ ORDERS : places
    ORDERS ||--|{ ORDER_ITEMS : contains
    PRODUCTS ||--o{ ORDER_ITEMS : ordered_product
    USERS o|--o{ ORDERS : creates
```

The database schema and foreign keys in `server/database.sql` are the source of truth for exact column definitions and constraints.

=== README.md ===
# Batloung Fruits Distribution Management System

A full-stack React + Express + MySQL application. Forms submit data to the Express API, and the API writes business records to MySQL. The frontend fetches saved records back from the API; JSON is only used to send data between the frontend and backend.

## Forms and database-backed modules

- **Fruit Management:** create/edit/delete fruit types and product listings (fruit, box size, quantity per box, selling price, stock, and status).
- **Supplier Management:** save supplier name, province, contact person, telephone, email, and physical address.
- **Street Vendor Management:** save vendor, business, telephone, area, registration date, and status.
- **Stock Management:** update stock and record purchases with supplier, product, quantity, cost, date, and notes. The purchase form stores a purchase record and increases available stock. Saving a purchase adds a row to `purchases` and increases product stock in a database transaction.
- **Orders:** create orders for vendors with one or more products and quantities; view details and update order status.
- **Reports and Dashboard:** display totals derived from database records.

## Requirements

- Node.js and npm
- MySQL Server 8 or compatible

## Setup

1. Open the project folder in PowerShell or VS Code.
2. Create a database named `batloung_fruits` in MySQL. For a fresh database, import `server/database.sql`, then import `server/initial_reference_data.sql` to add the province and box-size dropdown options. **Do not import `server/database.sql` over a database with records you need to keep without taking a backup first.** If you are using an existing database, run only `server/initial_reference_data.sql` if provinces or box sizes are missing; it uses `INSERT IGNORE` to preserve existing rows.
3. Copy `.env.example` to `.env`. On Windows PowerShell, run `Copy-Item .env.example .env`, then edit `.env` and enter  own MySQL password. Never share or upload  real `.env` file.
4. Install packages: `npm install`.
5. Start the backend in Terminal 1: `npm run server`. It should report a successful MySQL connection and listen on port 5001.
6. Start the frontend in Terminal 2: `npm run dev`. Open the local URL Vite prints (for example `http://localhost:5173/`; the port may change if busy).

## Verify the API

- Health check: `http://localhost:5001/api/health`
- Fruit types: `http://localhost:5001/api/fruits`
- Products: `http://localhost:5001/api/products`
- Suppliers: `http://localhost:5001/api/suppliers`
- Vendors: `http://localhost:5001/api/vendors`
- Stock: `http://localhost:5001/api/stock`
- Purchases: `http://localhost:5001/api/purchases`
- Orders: `http://localhost:5001/api/orders`

To verify a form submission, save an entry in the website and refresh the relevant API endpoint. The returned record should appear there. You can also query the corresponding MySQL table directly.

## Order stock rule

Creating an order does not deduct stock. When an order is changed to `completed`, the server checks stock and deducts ordered quantities in a transaction. If stock is insufficient, completion is rejected. Cancelling an order that already deducted stock restores the quantities.

## Build

Run `npm run build` to build the frontend for production.

  `total_amount` decimal(10,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0.00',

  `stock_deducted` tinyint(1) NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',

  CONSTRAINT `chk_price` CHECK ((`selling_price` > 0)),

=== db.js ===

import mysql from "mysql2/promise";
import dotenv from "dotenv";

dotenv.config();

const db = mysql.createPool({
  host: process.env.DB_HOST || "localhost",
  user: process.env.DB_USER || "root",
  password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || "",
  database: process.env.DB_NAME || "batloung_fruits",
  waitForConnections: true,
  connectionLimit: 10,
  queueLimit: 0,
});

// Test the database connection
try {
  const connection = await db.getConnection();
  console.log("Connected to Batloung Fruits MySQL database!");
  connection.release();
} catch (error) {
  console.error("MySQL connection failed:", error.message);
}

export default db;

=== initial_reference_data.sql ===
-- Safe, repeatable reference data for forms that use dropdowns.
-- Run after server/database.sql when setting up a new database.
-- INSERT IGNORE preserves any existing rows with the same unique name.

INSERT IGNORE INTO provinces (id, name) VALUES
  (1, 'Eastern Cape'),
  (2, 'Free State'),
  (3, 'Gauteng'),
  (4, 'KwaZulu-Natal'),
  (5, 'Limpopo'),
  (6, 'Mpumalanga'),
  (7, 'Northern Cape'),
  (8, 'North West'),
  (9, 'Western Cape');

INSERT IGNORE INTO box_sizes (id, size_name, description) VALUES
  (1, 'Small', 'Small box for lower-volume orders'),
  (2, 'Medium', 'Medium box for regular orders'),
  (3, 'Large', 'Large box for higher-volume orders');


=== reference_data.sql ===
/* =========================================================================
   Reference data migration
   Moves previously hardcoded application data (order statuses and the
   sidebar navigation menu) into the database so the frontend reads it
   from MySQL instead of static arrays.
   Safe to run more than once.
   ========================================================================= */

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `order_statuses` (
  `id` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `code` varchar(30) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL,
  `label` varchar(60) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL,
  `sort_order` int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `code` (`code`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

INSERT INTO `order_statuses` (`code`, `label`, `sort_order`) VALUES
  ('pending', 'Pending', 1),
  ('confirmed', 'Confirmed', 2),
  ('processing', 'Processing', 3),
  ('ready_for_collection', 'Ready for Collection/Delivery', 4),
  ('completed', 'Completed', 5),
  ('cancelled', 'Cancelled', 6)
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
  `label` = VALUES(`label`),
  `sort_order` = VALUES(`sort_order`);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `menu_items` (
  `id` int NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
  `name` varchar(60) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL,
  `path` varchar(120) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL,
  `icon` varchar(20) COLLATE utf8mb4_unicode_ci NOT NULL,
  `sort_order` int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`),
  UNIQUE KEY `path` (`path`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8mb4 COLLATE=utf8mb4_unicode_ci;

INSERT INTO `menu_items` (`name`, `path`, `icon`, `sort_order`) VALUES
  ('Dashboard', '/', 'ðŸ ', 1),
  ('Fruits', '/fruits', 'ðŸŽ', 2),
  ('Suppliers', '/suppliers', 'ðŸšš', 3),
  ('Street Vendors', '/vendors', 'ðŸª', 4),
  ('Stock Management', '/stock', 'ðŸ“¦', 5),
  ('Orders', '/orders', 'ðŸ›’', 6),
  ('Reports', '/reports', 'ðŸ“Š', 7)
ON DUPLICATE KEY UPDATE
  `name` = VALUES(`name`),
  `icon` = VALUES(`icon`),
  `sort_order` = VALUES(`sort_order`);


=== server.js ===

import express from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import dotenv from "dotenv";
import db from "./db.js";

dotenv.config();

const app = express();
const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5001;

app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());

// ==================================================
// HOME AND HEALTH
// ==================================================

app.get("/", (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    message: "Batloung Fruits Management API is running!",
  });
});

app.get("/api/health", (req, res) => {
  res.json({
    status: "success",
    message: "The server is working correctly.",
  });
});

// ==================================================
// FRUIT MANAGEMENT
// ==================================================

// Get all fruits
app.get("/api/fruits", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [fruits] = await db.query(
      `SELECT id, fruit_name, description, status
       FROM fruits ORDER BY id`
    );
    res.json(fruits);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching fruits:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to fetch fruits." });
  }
});

// Get one fruit
app.get("/api/fruits/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [rows] = await db.execute(
      `SELECT id, fruit_name, description, status
       FROM fruits WHERE id = ?`,
      [req.params.id]
    );

    if (!rows.length) {
      return res.status(404).json({ message: "Fruit not found." });
    }

    res.json(rows[0]);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching fruit:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({ message: "Failed to fetch fruit." });
  }
});

// Add a fruit
app.post("/api/fruits", async (req, res) => {
  const fruitName =
    typeof req.body.fruit_name === "string"
      ? req.body.fruit_name.trim()
      : "";
  const description =
    typeof req.body.description === "string"
      ? req.body.description.trim()
      : "";
  const status = req.body.status || "active";

  if (!fruitName) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message: "Fruit name is required.",
    });
  }

  if (fruitName.length > 60 || description.length > 255) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message:
        "Fruit name must be 60 characters or fewer and description 255 characters or fewer.",
    });
  }

  if (!["active", "inactive"].includes(status)) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message: "Status must be active or inactive.",
    });
  }

  try {
    const [result] = await db.execute(
      `INSERT INTO fruits (fruit_name, description, status)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?)`,
      [fruitName, description || null, status]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Fruit type added successfully.",
      id: result.insertId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ER_DUP_ENTRY") {
      return res.status(409).json({
        message: "This fruit already exists.",
      });
    }

    console.error("Error adding fruit:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to add fruit type.",
    });
  }
});

// Update a fruit
app.put("/api/fruits/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = Number(req.params.id);
  const fruitName =
    typeof req.body.fruit_name === "string"
      ? req.body.fruit_name.trim()
      : "";
  const description =
    typeof req.body.description === "string"
      ? req.body.description.trim()
      : "";
  const status = req.body.status || "active";

  if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({ message: "Invalid fruit ID." });
  }

  if (!fruitName) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message: "Fruit name is required.",
    });
  }

  if (fruitName.length > 60 || description.length > 255) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message:
        "Fruit name must be 60 characters or fewer and description 255 characters or fewer.",
    });
  }

  if (!["active", "inactive"].includes(status)) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message: "Status must be active or inactive.",
    });
  }

  try {
    const [result] = await db.execute(
      `UPDATE fruits
       SET fruit_name = ?, description = ?, status = ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [fruitName, description || null, status, id]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      const [rows] = await db.execute(
        "SELECT id FROM fruits WHERE id = ?",
        [id]
      );

      if (!rows.length) {
        return res.status(404).json({
          message: "Fruit type not found.",
        });
      }
    }

    res.json({ message: "Fruit type updated successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ER_DUP_ENTRY") {
      return res.status(409).json({
        message: "This fruit already exists.",
      });
    }

    console.error("Error updating fruit:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to update fruit type.",
    });
  }
});

// Delete a fruit
app.delete("/api/fruits/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [result] = await db.execute(
      "DELETE FROM fruits WHERE id = ?",
      [req.params.id]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Fruit not found.",
      });
    }

    res.json({ message: "Fruit deleted successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error deleting fruit:", error.message);
    res.status(409).json({
      message:
        "This fruit cannot be deleted because it may be linked to other records.",
    });
  }
});

    res.json(sizes);

    console.error("Error fetching box sizes:", error.message);

    });
  }
});

// Get all products
app.get("/api/products", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [products] = await db.execute(`
      SELECT
        p.id,
        p.fruit_id,
        f.fruit_name AS fruit,
        p.box_size_id,
        b.size_name AS size,
        p.quantity_per_box,
        p.selling_price,
        p.quantity_available,
        p.status
      FROM products p
      JOIN fruits f ON p.fruit_id = f.id
      JOIN box_sizes b ON p.box_size_id = b.id
      ORDER BY p.id
    `);
    res.json(products);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching products:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch products.",
    });
  }
});

// Add a product
app.post("/api/products", async (req, res) => {
  const {
    fruit_id,
    box_size_id,
    quantity_per_box = 10,
    selling_price,
    quantity_available = 0,
    status = "active",
  } = req.body;

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(Number(fruit_id)) ||
    Number(fruit_id) <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(Number(box_size_id)) ||
    Number(box_size_id) <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(Number(quantity_per_box)) ||
    Number(quantity_per_box) <= 0 ||
    selling_price === "" ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(selling_price)) ||
    Number(selling_price) <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(Number(quantity_available)) ||
    Number(quantity_available) < 0 ||
    !["active", "inactive"].includes(status)
  ) {
    return res.status(400).json({
      message: "Please enter valid product details.",
    });
  }

  try {
    const [result] = await db.execute(
      `INSERT INTO products
       (fruit_id, box_size_id, quantity_per_box, selling_price,
        quantity_available, status)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        Number(fruit_id),
        Number(box_size_id),
        Number(quantity_per_box),
        Number(selling_price),
        Number(quantity_available),
        status,
      ]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Product added successfully.",
      id: result.insertId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ER_DUP_ENTRY") {
      return res.status(409).json({
        message: "This fruit and box size already have a product.",
      });
    }

    if (error.code === "ER_NO_REFERENCED_ROW_2") {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "The selected fruit or box size does not exist.",
      });
    }

    console.error("Error adding product:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to add product.",
    });
  }
});

// Update a product
app.put("/api/products/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = Number(req.params.id);
  const {
    fruit_id,
    box_size_id,
    quantity_per_box,
    selling_price,
    quantity_available,

  } = req.body;

  if (
    !Number.isInteger(id) ||

    Number(fruit_id) <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(Number(box_size_id)) ||
    Number(box_size_id) <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(Number(quantity_per_box)) ||
    Number(quantity_per_box) <= 0 ||
    selling_price === "" ||
    !Number.isFinite(Number(selling_price)) ||
    Number(selling_price) <= 0 ||
    !Number.isInteger(Number(quantity_available)) ||

    !["active", "inactive"].includes(status)
  ) {
    return res.status(400).json({

    });

       SET fruit_id = ?, box_size_id = ?, quantity_per_box = ?,

       WHERE id = ?`,

      ]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      const [rows] = await db.execute(

      if (!rows.length) {
        return res.status(404).json({
          message: "Product not found.",
        });

    }

    res.json({ message: "Product updated successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ER_DUP_ENTRY") {
      return res.status(409).json({

      });
    }

    if (error.code === "ER_NO_REFERENCED_ROW_2") {

// Get all provinces
app.get("/api/provinces", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [provinces] = await db.query(
      "SELECT id, name FROM provinces ORDER BY name"
    );
    res.json(provinces);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching provinces:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch provinces.",
    });
  }
});

// Add a province
app.post("/api/provinces", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const name =
      typeof req.body.name === "string"
        ? req.body.name.trim()
        : "";

    if (!name) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Province name is required.",
      });
    }

    if (name.length > 100) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Province name must not exceed 100 characters.",
      });
    }

    const [existing] = await db.execute(
      "SELECT id FROM provinces WHERE LOWER(name) = LOWER(?)",
      [name]
    );

    if (existing.length) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        message: "This province already exists.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.execute(
      "INSERT INTO provinces (name) VALUES (?)",
      [name]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Province added successfully.",
      id: result.insertId,
      name,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error adding province:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to add province.",
    });
  }
});

// Get all suppliers
app.get("/api/suppliers", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [suppliers] = await db.query(`
      SELECT
        s.id,
        s.supplier_name,
        s.province_id,
        p.name AS province,
        s.contact_person,
        s.telephone,
        s.email,
        s.physical_address,
        s.created_at
      FROM suppliers s
      JOIN provinces p ON s.province_id = p.id
      ORDER BY s.id DESC
    `);
    res.json(suppliers);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching suppliers:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch suppliers.",
    });
  }
});

// Get one supplier
app.get("/api/suppliers/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid supplier ID.",
      });
    }

    const [rows] = await db.query(
      `SELECT s.id, s.supplier_name, s.province_id,
              p.name AS province, s.contact_person, s.telephone,
              s.email, s.physical_address, s.created_at
       FROM suppliers s
       JOIN provinces p ON s.province_id = p.id
       WHERE s.id = ?`,
      [id]
    );

    if (!rows.length) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Supplier not found.",
      });
    }

    res.json(rows[0]);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching supplier:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch supplier.",
    });
  }
});

// Add a supplier
app.post("/api/suppliers", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      supplier_name,
      province_id,
      contact_person,
      telephone,
      email,
      physical_address,
    } = req.body;

    if (
      typeof supplier_name !== "string" ||
      !supplier_name.trim() ||
      !province_id ||
      typeof contact_person !== "string" ||
      !contact_person.trim() ||
      typeof telephone !== "string" ||
      !telephone.trim() ||
      typeof email !== "string" ||
      !email.trim() ||
      typeof physical_address !== "string" ||
      !physical_address.trim()
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please provide all required supplier details.",
      });
    }

    const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

    if (!emailPattern.test(email.trim())) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please provide a valid email address.",
      });
    }

    const [provinceRows] = await db.query(
      "SELECT id FROM provinces WHERE id = ?",
      [Number(province_id)]
    );

    if (!provinceRows.length) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please select a valid province.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO suppliers
       (supplier_name, province_id, contact_person, telephone,
        email, physical_address)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        supplier_name.trim(),
        Number(province_id),
        contact_person.trim(),
        telephone.trim(),
        email.trim(),
        physical_address.trim(),
      ]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Supplier added successfully.",
      id: result.insertId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error adding supplier:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to add supplier.",
    });
  }
});

// Update a supplier
app.put("/api/suppliers/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);
    const {
      supplier_name,
      province_id,
      contact_person,
      telephone,
      email,
      physical_address,
    } = req.body;

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid supplier ID.",
      });
    }

    if (
      typeof supplier_name !== "string" ||
      !supplier_name.trim() ||
      !province_id ||
      typeof contact_person !== "string" ||
      !contact_person.trim() ||
      typeof telephone !== "string" ||
      !telephone.trim() ||
      typeof email !== "string" ||
      !email.trim() ||
      typeof physical_address !== "string" ||
      !physical_address.trim()
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please provide all required supplier details.",
      });
    }

    const emailPattern = /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;

    if (!emailPattern.test(email.trim())) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please provide a valid email address.",
      });
    }

    const [provinceRows] = await db.query(
      "SELECT id FROM provinces WHERE id = ?",
      [Number(province_id)]
    );

    if (!provinceRows.length) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please select a valid province.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.query(
      `UPDATE suppliers
       SET supplier_name = ?, province_id = ?, contact_person = ?,
           telephone = ?, email = ?, physical_address = ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [
        supplier_name.trim(),
        Number(province_id),
        contact_person.trim(),
        telephone.trim(),
        email.trim(),
        physical_address.trim(),
        id,
      ]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      const [rows] = await db.query(
        "SELECT id FROM suppliers WHERE id = ?",
        [id]
      );

      if (!rows.length) {
        return res.status(404).json({
          message: "Supplier not found.",
        });
      }
    }

    res.json({ message: "Supplier updated successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating supplier:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to update supplier.",
    });
  }
});

// Delete a supplier
app.delete("/api/suppliers/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid supplier ID.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.query(
      "DELETE FROM suppliers WHERE id = ?",
      [id]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Supplier not found.",
      });
    }

    res.json({ message: "Supplier deleted successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ER_ROW_IS_REFERENCED_2") {
      return res.status(409).json({
        message:
          "This supplier cannot be deleted because it has linked purchases.",
      });
    }

    console.error("Error deleting supplier:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to delete supplier.",
    });
  }
});

    res.json(vendors);

// Get one vendor
app.get("/api/vendors/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid vendor ID.",
      });
    }

    const [rows] = await db.query(
      `SELECT id, vendor_name, business_name, telephone, area,
              registration_date, status, created_at
       FROM street_vendors WHERE id = ?`,
      [id]
    );

    if (!rows.length) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Street vendor not found.",
      });
    }

    res.json(rows[0]);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching vendor:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch street vendor.",
    });
  }
});

// Add a vendor
app.post("/api/vendors", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const {
      vendor_name,
      business_name,
      telephone,
      area,
      registration_date,
      status = "active",
    } = req.body;

    if (
      typeof vendor_name !== "string" ||
      !vendor_name.trim() ||
      typeof business_name !== "string" ||
      !business_name.trim() ||
      typeof telephone !== "string" ||
      !telephone.trim() ||
      typeof area !== "string" ||
      !area.trim()
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please complete all required vendor details.",
      });
    }

    if (!["active", "inactive"].includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Status must be active or inactive.",
      });
    }

    let date = null;

    if (registration_date) {
      if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(registration_date)) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          message: "Registration date must use YYYY-MM-DD format.",
        });
      }
      date = registration_date;
    }

    const [result] = await db.query(
      `INSERT INTO street_vendors
       (vendor_name, business_name, telephone, area,
        registration_date, status)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, COALESCE(?, CURDATE()), ?)`,
      [
        vendor_name.trim(),
        business_name.trim(),
        telephone.trim(),
        area.trim(),
        date,
        status,
      ]
    );

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Street vendor added successfully.",
      id: result.insertId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error adding vendor:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to add vendor.",
    });
  }
});

// Update a vendor
app.put("/api/vendors/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);
    const {
      vendor_name,
      business_name,
      telephone,
      area,
      registration_date,
      status,
    } = req.body;

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid vendor ID.",
      });
    }

    if (
      typeof vendor_name !== "string" ||
      !vendor_name.trim() ||
      typeof business_name !== "string" ||
      !business_name.trim() ||
      typeof telephone !== "string" ||
      !telephone.trim() ||
      typeof area !== "string" ||
      !area.trim() ||
      !registration_date
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Please complete all required vendor details.",
      });
    }

    if (!["active", "inactive"].includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Status must be active or inactive.",
      });
    }

    if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(registration_date)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Registration date must use YYYY-MM-DD format.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.query(
      `UPDATE street_vendors
       SET vendor_name = ?, business_name = ?, telephone = ?,
           area = ?, registration_date = ?, status = ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [
        vendor_name.trim(),
        business_name.trim(),
        telephone.trim(),
        area.trim(),
        registration_date,
        status,
        id,
      ]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      const [rows] = await db.query(
        "SELECT id FROM street_vendors WHERE id = ?",
        [id]
      );

      if (!rows.length) {
        return res.status(404).json({
          message: "Street vendor not found.",
        });
      }
    }

    res.json({ message: "Street vendor updated successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating vendor:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to update vendor.",
    });
  }
});

// Delete a vendor
app.delete("/api/vendors/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(id) || id <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid vendor ID.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.query(
      "DELETE FROM street_vendors WHERE id = ?",
      [id]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Street vendor not found.",
      });
    }

    res.json({ message: "Street vendor deleted successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === "ER_ROW_IS_REFERENCED_2") {
      return res.status(409).json({
        message:
          "This vendor cannot be deleted because it has linked orders.",
      });
    }

    console.error("Error deleting vendor:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to delete vendor.",
    });
  }
});

// Get all orders
app.get("/api/orders", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [orders] = await db.query(`
      SELECT o.id, o.order_number, o.vendor_id,
             v.vendor_name, v.business_name,
             o.order_date, o.status, o.total_amount,
             o.notes, o.created_at
      FROM orders o
      JOIN street_vendors v ON o.vendor_id = v.id
      ORDER BY o.id DESC
    `);
    res.json(orders);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching orders:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch orders.",
    });
  }
});

// Get one order and its items
app.get("/api/orders/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const orderId = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(orderId) || orderId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid order ID.",
      });
    }

    const [orders] = await db.execute(
      `SELECT o.*, v.vendor_name, v.business_name, v.telephone
       FROM orders o
       JOIN street_vendors v ON o.vendor_id = v.id
       WHERE o.id = ?`,
      [orderId]
    );

    if (!orders.length) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Order not found.",
      });
    }

    const [items] = await db.execute(
      `SELECT oi.id, oi.product_id, f.fruit_name AS fruit,
              b.size_name AS size, oi.quantity_boxes,
              oi.unit_price, oi.subtotal
       FROM order_items oi
       JOIN products p ON oi.product_id = p.id
       JOIN fruits f ON p.fruit_id = f.id
       JOIN box_sizes b ON p.box_size_id = b.id
       WHERE oi.order_id = ?
       ORDER BY oi.id`,
      [orderId]
    );

    res.json({ ...orders[0], items });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching order:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch order.",
    });
  }
});

// Create an order
app.post("/api/orders", async (req, res) => {
  let connection;

  try {
    const vendorId = Number(req.body.vendor_id);
    const { items, notes = null } = req.body;

    if (!Number.isInteger(vendorId) || vendorId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Select a valid vendor.",
      });
    }

    if (!Array.isArray(items) || !items.length) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "An order must contain at least one product.",
      });
    }

    if (
      notes !== null &&
      (typeof notes !== "string" || notes.length > 255)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Notes must be 255 characters or fewer.",
      });
    }

    const cleanItems = items.map((item) => ({
      product_id: Number(item.product_id),
      quantity_boxes: Number(item.quantity_boxes),
    }));

    if (
      cleanItems.some(
        (item) =>
          !Number.isInteger(item.product_id) ||
          item.product_id <= 0 ||
          !Number.isInteger(item.quantity_boxes) ||
          item.quantity_boxes <= 0
      )
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Each product needs a valid ID and positive box quantity.",
      });
    }

    const quantities = new Map();

    for (const item of cleanItems) {
      quantities.set(
        item.product_id,
        (quantities.get(item.product_id) || 0) +
          item.quantity_boxes
      );
    }

    const orderItems = Array.from(
      quantities,
      ([product_id, quantity_boxes]) => ({
        product_id,
        quantity_boxes,
      })
    );

    connection = await db.getConnection();
    await connection.beginTransaction();

    const [vendors] = await connection.execute(
      `SELECT id FROM street_vendors
       WHERE id = ? AND status = 'active'`,
      [vendorId]
    );

    if (!vendors.length) {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Vendor not found or inactive.",
      });
    }

    const year = new Date().getFullYear();

    const [numberRows] = await connection.execute(
      `SELECT COALESCE(
         MAX(CAST(SUBSTRING_INDEX(order_number, '-', -1) AS UNSIGNED)),
         0
       ) AS last_number
       FROM orders
       WHERE order_number LIKE ?`,
      [`ORD-${year}-%`]
    );

    const nextNumber = Number(numberRows[0].last_number) + 1;
    const orderNumber =
      `ORD-${year}-${String(nextNumber).padStart(4, "0")}`;

    const [orderResult] = await connection.execute(
      `INSERT INTO orders
       (order_number, vendor_id, status, total_amount, notes, stock_deducted)
       VALUES (?, ?, 'pending', 0, ?, 0)`,
      [orderNumber, vendorId, notes]
    );

    const orderId = orderResult.insertId;
    let totalAmount = 0;

    for (const item of orderItems) {
      const [products] = await connection.execute(
        `SELECT id, selling_price, quantity_available, status
         FROM products WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE`,
        [item.product_id]
      );

      if (!products.length || products[0].status !== "active") {
        throw new Error(
          `Product ${item.product_id} does not exist or is inactive.`
        );
      }

      const product = products[0];

      if (
        Number(product.quantity_available) <
        item.quantity_boxes
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          `Insufficient stock for product ${item.product_id}. ` +
          `Available: ${product.quantity_available} boxes.`
        );
      }

      const unitPrice = Number(product.selling_price);
      const subtotal = unitPrice * item.quantity_boxes;

      await connection.execute(
        `INSERT INTO order_items
         (order_id, product_id, quantity_boxes, unit_price, subtotal)
         VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
        [
          orderId,
          item.product_id,
          item.quantity_boxes,
          unitPrice,
          subtotal,
        ]
      );

      totalAmount += subtotal;
    }

    await connection.execute(
      `UPDATE orders
       SET total_amount = ?, stock_deducted = 0
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [totalAmount, orderId]
    );

    await connection.commit();

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Order created successfully.",
      id: orderId,
      order_number: orderNumber,
      total_amount: totalAmount,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (connection) await connection.rollback();

    console.error("Error creating order:", error.message);

    const clientError =
      error.message.startsWith("Insufficient stock") ||
      error.message.startsWith("Product ") ||
      error.message.startsWith("Stock changed");

    res.status(clientError ? 400 : 500).json({
      message: clientError
        ? error.message
        : "Failed to create order.",
    });
  } finally {
    if (connection) connection.release();
  }
});

// Update an order's vendor or notes
app.put("/api/orders/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const orderId = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(orderId) || orderId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid order ID.",
      });
    }

    const [orders] = await db.query(
      "SELECT id, status FROM orders WHERE id = ?",
      [orderId]
    );

    if (!orders.length) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Order not found.",
      });
    }

    if (["completed", "cancelled"].includes(orders[0].status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Completed or cancelled orders cannot be edited.",
      });
    }

    const { vendor_id, notes } = req.body;

    if (
      notes !== undefined &&
      notes !== null &&
      (typeof notes !== "string" || notes.length > 255)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Notes must be 255 characters or fewer.",
      });
    }

    if (vendor_id !== undefined) {
      const vendorId = Number(vendor_id);

      if (!Number.isInteger(vendorId) || vendorId <= 0) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          message: "Select a valid vendor.",
        });
      }

      const [vendors] = await db.query(
        `SELECT id FROM street_vendors
         WHERE id = ? AND status = 'active'`,
        [vendorId]
      );

      if (!vendors.length) {
        return res.status(400).json({
          message: "Vendor not found or inactive.",
        });
      }

      await db.query(
        "UPDATE orders SET vendor_id = ?, notes = ? WHERE id = ?",
        [
          vendorId,
          notes === undefined ? null : notes,
          orderId,
        ]
      );
    } else if (notes !== undefined) {
      await db.query(
        "UPDATE orders SET notes = ? WHERE id = ?",
        [notes, orderId]
      );
    } else {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Provide vendor_id or notes to update.",
      });
    }

    res.json({
      message: "Order updated successfully.",
      id: orderId,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating order:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to update order.",
    });
  }
});

// Delete an order that has not been completed
app.delete("/api/orders/:id", async (req, res) => {
  let connection;

  try {
    const orderId = Number(req.params.id);

    if (!Number.isInteger(orderId) || orderId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid order ID.",
      });
    }

    connection = await db.getConnection();
    await connection.beginTransaction();

    const [orders] = await connection.execute(
      "SELECT * FROM orders WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE",
      [orderId]
    );

    if (!orders.length) {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Order not found.",
      });
    }

    const order = orders[0];

    if (order.status === "completed") {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Completed orders cannot be deleted.",
      });
    }

    if (Number(order.stock_deducted) === 1) {
      const [items] = await connection.execute(
        `SELECT product_id, quantity_boxes
         FROM order_items WHERE order_id = ?`,
        [orderId]
      );

      for (const item of items) {
        await connection.execute(
          `UPDATE products
           SET quantity_available = quantity_available + ?
           WHERE id = ?`,
          [item.quantity_boxes, item.product_id]
        );
      }
    }

    await connection.execute(
      "DELETE FROM orders WHERE id = ?",
      [orderId]
    );

    await connection.commit();

    res.json({ message: "Order deleted successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    if (connection) await connection.rollback();

    console.error("Error deleting order:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to delete order.",
    });
  } finally {
    if (connection) connection.release();
  }
});

// Update order status and handle stock
app.patch("/api/orders/:id/status", async (req, res) => {
  let connection;

  try {
    const orderId = Number(req.params.id);
    const { status } = req.body;

    if (!Number.isInteger(orderId) || orderId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid order ID.",
      });
    }

    const [statusRows] = await db.query(
      "SELECT code FROM order_statuses"
    );

    const allowedStatuses = statusRows.map((row) => row.code);

    if (!allowedStatuses.includes(status)) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid order status.",
      });
    }

    connection = await db.getConnection();
    await connection.beginTransaction();

    const [orders] = await connection.execute(
      "SELECT * FROM orders WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE",
      [orderId]
    );

    if (!orders.length) {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Order not found.",
      });
    }

    const order = orders[0];

    if (order.status === "cancelled") {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "A cancelled order cannot be changed.",
      });
    }

    if (order.status === "completed" && status !== "completed") {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Completed orders cannot be changed.",
      });
    }

    // Deduct stock when completing an order
    if (status === "completed" && Number(order.stock_deducted) !== 1) {
      const [items] = await connection.execute(
        `SELECT product_id, quantity_boxes
         FROM order_items WHERE order_id = ?`,
        [orderId]
      );

      for (const item of items) {
        const [products] = await connection.execute(
          `SELECT quantity_available
           FROM products WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE`,
          [item.product_id]
        );

        if (
          !products.length ||
          Number(products[0].quantity_available) <
            Number(item.quantity_boxes)
        ) {
          await connection.rollback();
          return res.status(400).json({
            message:
              `Insufficient stock to complete this order for product ${item.product_id}.`,
          });
        }

        const [deductResult] = await connection.execute(
          `UPDATE products
           SET quantity_available = quantity_available - ?
           WHERE id = ? AND quantity_available >= ?`,
          [
            item.quantity_boxes,
            item.product_id,
            item.quantity_boxes,
          ]
        );

        if (deductResult.affectedRows !== 1) {
          throw new Error(
            `Stock changed while completing product ${item.product_id}.`
          );
        }
      }

      await connection.execute(
        "UPDATE orders SET stock_deducted = 1 WHERE id = ?",
        [orderId]
      );
    }

    // Restore stock if a deducted order is cancelled
    if (status === "cancelled" && Number(order.stock_deducted) === 1) {
      const [items] = await connection.execute(
        `SELECT product_id, quantity_boxes
         FROM order_items WHERE order_id = ?`,
        [orderId]
      );

      for (const item of items) {
        await connection.execute(
          `UPDATE products
           SET quantity_available = quantity_available + ?
           WHERE id = ?`,
          [item.quantity_boxes, item.product_id]
        );
      }

      await connection.execute(
        "UPDATE orders SET stock_deducted = 0 WHERE id = ?",
        [orderId]
      );
    }

    await connection.execute(
      "UPDATE orders SET status = ? WHERE id = ?",
      [status, orderId]
    );

    await connection.commit();

    res.json({
      message: "Order status updated successfully.",
      id: orderId,
      status,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (connection) await connection.rollback();

    console.error("Error updating order status:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to update order status.",
    });
  } finally {
    if (connection) connection.release();
  }
});

// Get all purchases
app.get("/api/purchases", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [purchases] = await db.query(`
      SELECT
        pu.id,
        pu.supplier_id,
        s.supplier_name,
        pr.id AS product_id,
        f.fruit_name AS fruit,
        b.size_name AS box_size,
        pu.quantity_boxes,
        pu.unit_cost,
        pu.total_cost,
        pu.purchase_date,
        pu.notes,
        pu.created_at
      FROM purchases pu
      JOIN suppliers s ON pu.supplier_id = s.id
      JOIN products pr ON pu.product_id = pr.id
      JOIN fruits f ON pr.fruit_id = f.id
      JOIN box_sizes b ON pr.box_size_id = b.id
      ORDER BY pu.id DESC
    `);
    res.json(purchases);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching purchases:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch purchases.",
    });
  }
});

// Record a purchase and increase stock
app.post("/api/purchases", async (req, res) => {
  let connection;

  try {
    const supplierId = Number(req.body.supplier_id);
    const productId = Number(req.body.product_id);
    const quantity = Number(req.body.quantity_boxes);
    const unitCost = Number(req.body.unit_cost);
    const purchaseDate = req.body.purchase_date;
    const notes = req.body.notes ?? null;

    if (
      !Number.isInteger(supplierId) ||
      supplierId <= 0 ||
      !Number.isInteger(productId) ||
      productId <= 0 ||
      !Number.isInteger(quantity) ||
      quantity <= 0 ||
      !Number.isFinite(unitCost) ||
      unitCost < 0
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message:
          "Enter a valid supplier, product, positive box quantity, and non-negative unit cost.",
      });
    }

    if (
      !purchaseDate ||
      !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(purchaseDate) ||
      Number.isNaN(Date.parse(purchaseDate))
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Enter a valid purchase date in YYYY-MM-DD format.",
      });
    }

    if (
      notes !== null &&
      (typeof notes !== "string" || notes.length > 255)
    ) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Notes must be 255 characters or fewer.",
      });
    }

    const totalCost = Number((quantity * unitCost).toFixed(2));

    connection = await db.getConnection();
    await connection.beginTransaction();

    const [suppliers] = await connection.execute(
      "SELECT id FROM suppliers WHERE id = ?",
      [supplierId]
    );

    if (!suppliers.length) {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Selected supplier does not exist.",
      });
    }

    const [products] = await connection.execute(
      `SELECT id, status FROM products
       WHERE id = ? FOR UPDATE`,
      [productId]
    );

    if (!products.length || products[0].status !== "active") {
      await connection.rollback();
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Selected product does not exist or is inactive.",
      });
    }

    const [purchaseResult] = await connection.execute(
      `INSERT INTO purchases
       (supplier_id, product_id, quantity_boxes, unit_cost,
        total_cost, purchase_date, notes)
       VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)`,
      [
        supplierId,
        productId,
        quantity,
        unitCost,
        totalCost,
        purchaseDate,
        notes,
      ]
    );

    await connection.execute(
      `UPDATE products
       SET quantity_available = quantity_available + ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [quantity, productId]
    );

    await connection.commit();

    res.status(201).json({
      message: "Purchase recorded and stock updated successfully.",
      id: purchaseResult.insertId,
      total_cost: totalCost,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (connection) await connection.rollback();

    console.error("Error recording purchase:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to record purchase.",
    });
  } finally {
    if (connection) connection.release();
  }
});

// Get available stock
app.get("/api/stock", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [stock] = await db.query(`
      SELECT
        p.id AS product_id,
        f.fruit_name AS fruit,
        b.size_name AS box_size,
        p.quantity_per_box,
        p.selling_price,
        p.quantity_available,
        p.status
      FROM products p
      JOIN fruits f ON p.fruit_id = f.id
      JOIN box_sizes b ON p.box_size_id = b.id
      ORDER BY f.fruit_name, b.size_name
    `);
    res.json(stock);
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error fetching stock:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to fetch stock.",
    });
  }
});

// Update available stock quantity
app.put("/api/stock/:id", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const productId = Number(req.params.id);
    const quantity = Number(req.body.quantity_available);

    if (!Number.isInteger(productId) || productId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid product ID.",
      });
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Quantity must be a non-negative whole number.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.execute(
      `UPDATE products SET quantity_available = ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [quantity, productId]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      const [rows] = await db.execute(
        "SELECT id FROM products WHERE id = ?",
        [productId]
      );

      if (!rows.length) {
        return res.status(404).json({
          message: "Product not found.",
        });
      }
    }

    res.json({ message: "Stock updated successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error updating stock:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to update stock.",
    });
  }
});

// Add boxes to available stock
app.post("/api/stock", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const productId = Number(req.body.product_id);
    const quantity = Number(req.body.quantity_boxes);

    if (!Number.isInteger(productId) || productId <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Valid product ID is required.",
      });
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity <= 0) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Quantity must be a positive whole number.",
      });
    }

    const [result] = await db.execute(
      `UPDATE products
       SET quantity_available = quantity_available + ?
       WHERE id = ?`,
      [quantity, productId]
    );

    if (!result.affectedRows) {
      const [rows] = await db.execute(
        "SELECT id FROM products WHERE id = ?",
        [productId]
      );

      if (!rows.length) {
        return res.status(404).json({
          message: "Product not found.",
        });
      }
    }

    res.json({ message: "Stock increased successfully." });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Error adding stock:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Failed to add stock.",
    });
  }
});

// ==================================================
// DASHBOARD
// ==================================================

app.get("/api/dashboard", async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const [fruitRows] = await db.query(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM fruits"
    );

    const [supplierRows] = await db.query(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM suppliers"
    );

    const [vendorRows] = await db.query(
      "SELECT COUNT(*) AS total FROM street_vendors"
    );

    const [stockRows] = await db.query(`
      SELECT COALESCE(SUM(quantity_available), 0) AS total
      FROM products
    `);

    const [orderRows] = await db.query(`
      SELECT
        COUNT(*) AS total_orders,
        COALESCE(SUM(status = 'pending'), 0) AS pending_orders,
        COALESCE(SUM(status = 'completed'), 0) AS completed_orders,
        COALESCE(
          SUM(
            CASE
              WHEN status = 'completed' THEN total_amount
              ELSE 0
            END
          ),
          0
        ) AS sales_value
      FROM orders
    `);

    res.json({
      totalFruits: Number(fruitRows[0].total),
      totalSuppliers: Number(supplierRows[0].total),
      registeredVendors: Number(vendorRows[0].total),
      availableStock: Number(stockRows[0].total),
      totalOrders: Number(orderRows[0].total_orders),
      pendingOrders: Number(orderRows[0].pending_orders),
      completedOrders: Number(orderRows[0].completed_orders),
      salesValue: Number(orderRows[0].sales_value),
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Dashboard error:", error.message);
    res.status(500).json({
      message: "Could not load dashboard statistics.",
    });
  }
});

// ==================================================
// START SERVER â€” KEEP THIS AT THE VERY BOTTOM
// ==================================================

app.listen(PORT, () => {
  console.log(
    `Batloung Fruits server running at http://localhost:${PORT}`
  );
});

=== App.jsx ===
import Orders from "./Orders";
import Reports from "./pages/Reports";
import Fruits from "./pages/Fruits";
import Suppliers from "./pages/Suppliers";
import Vendors from "./pages/Vendors";
import Stock from "./pages/Stock";
import { BrowserRouter, Routes, Route } from "react-router-dom";
import Layout from "./components/Layout";
import Dashboard from "./pages/Dashboard";
import "./App.css";

// Temporary pages until we build each module
function PlaceholderPage({ title }) {
  return (
    <div className="placeholder-page">
      <h1>{title}</h1>
      <p>This section is being prepared.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<Layout />}>
        <Route path="vendors" element={<Vendors />} />
          <Route index element={<Dashboard />} />
          <Route path="fruits" element={<Fruits />} />
          <Route path="suppliers" element={<Suppliers />} />
          <Route path="stock" element={<Stock />} />
          <Route path="orders" element={<Orders />} />
          <Route path="reports" element={<Reports />} />
        </Route>
      </Routes>
    </BrowserRouter>
  );
}

export default App;

=== main.jsx ===

import { StrictMode } from "react";
import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
import "./index.css";

createRoot(document.getElementById("root")).render(
  <StrictMode>

=== Orders.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = "http://localhost:5001/api";

async function parseResponse(response, fallback) {
  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(
      data.message || data.error || fallback
    );
  }

  return data;
}

export default function Orders() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [vendors, setVendors] = useState([]);
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [orderStatuses, setOrderStatuses] = useState([]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");

  const [vendorId, setVendorId] = useState("");
  const [items, setItems] = useState([
    { product_id: "", quantity_boxes: "" },
  ]);
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");

  const [selectedOrder, setSelectedOrder] = useState(null);
  const [detailsLoading, setDetailsLoading] = useState(false);
  const [cancellingId, setCancellingId] = useState(null);
  const [updatingId, setUpdatingId] = useState(null);

  function formatStatus(status) {
    const match = orderStatuses.find(
      (item) => item.code === status
    );

    if (match) return match.label;

    return String(status || "")
      .replaceAll("_", " ")
      .replace(/\b\w/g, (letter) => letter.toUpperCase());
  }

  async function loadData() {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const responses = await Promise.all([
        fetch(`${API}/orders`),
        fetch(`${API}/vendors`),
        fetch(`${API}/products`),
        fetch(`${API}/order-statuses`),
      ]);

      const [
        ordersData,
        vendorsData,
        productsData,
        statusesData,
      ] = await Promise.all(
        responses.map((response) =>
          parseResponse(response, "Could not load order information.")
        )
      );

      setOrders(Array.isArray(ordersData) ? ordersData : []);
      setVendors(Array.isArray(vendorsData) ? vendorsData : []);
      setProducts(Array.isArray(productsData) ? productsData : []);
      setOrderStatuses(
        Array.isArray(statusesData) ? statusesData : []
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        `${err.message} Check that the backend is running on port 5001.`
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function updateOrderStatus(orderId, newStatus) {
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
    setUpdatingId(orderId);

    try {
      await parseResponse(
        await fetch(`${API}/orders/${orderId}/status`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({ status: newStatus }),
        }),
        "Failed to update order status."
      );

      setMessage(
        `Order status updated to ${formatStatus(newStatus)}.`
      );

      await loadData();
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage(err.message || "Could not update order status.");
      await loadData();
      return false;
    } finally {
      setUpdatingId(null);
    }
  }

  async function cancelOrder(order) {
    if (
      order.status === "completed" ||
      order.status === "cancelled"
    ) {
      setMessage(
        "Completed or already cancelled orders cannot be cancelled."
      );
      return;
    }

    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Are you sure you want to cancel ${order.order_number}?\n\nStock restoration will depend on  backend's cancellation logic.`
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setCancellingId(order.id);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    try {
      await parseResponse(
        await fetch(`${API}/orders/${order.id}/status`, {
          method: "PATCH",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({ status: "cancelled" }),
        }),
        "Could not cancel the order."
      );

      setMessage(`${order.order_number} was cancelled successfully.`);

      await loadData();

      if (selectedOrder?.id === order.id) {
        try {
          const detailsResponse = await fetch(
            `${API}/orders/${order.id}`
          );

          if (detailsResponse.ok) {
            const detailsData = await detailsResponse.json();
            setSelectedOrder(detailsData);
          }
        } catch {
          // The cancellation succeeded; details can be reopened.
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage(err.message || "Could not cancel the order.");
      await loadData();
    } finally {
      setCancellingId(null);
    }
  }

  async function viewOrderDetails(orderId) {
    setDetailsLoading(true);
    setSelectedOrder(null);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    try {
      const data = await parseResponse(
        await fetch(`${API}/orders/${orderId}`),
        "Failed to load order details."
      );

      setSelectedOrder(data);
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage(err.message || "Could not load order details.");
    } finally {
      setDetailsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function updateItem(index, field, value) {
    setItems((current) =>
      current.map((item, itemIndex) =>
        itemIndex === index
          ? { ...item, [field]: value }
          : item
      )
    );
  }

  function addItem() {
    setItems((current) => [
      ...current,
      { product_id: "", quantity_boxes: "" },
    ]);
  }

  function removeItem(index) {
    setItems((current) =>
      current.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index)
    );
  }

  function resetOrderForm() {
    setVendorId("");
    setItems([{ product_id: "", quantity_boxes: "" }]);
    setNotes("");
  }

  function getProductId(product) {
    return product.id ?? product.product_id;
  }

  function getProductName(product) {
    const fruit = product.fruit ?? product.fruit_name ?? "Fruit";
    const size =
      product.size ?? product.box_size ?? product.size_name ?? "Box";

    return `${fruit} â€” ${size}`;
  }

  const total = items.reduce((sum, item) => {
    const product = products.find(
      (productItem) =>
        String(getProductId(productItem)) ===
        String(item.product_id)
    );

    return (
      sum +
      (Number(product?.selling_price) || 0) *
        (Number(item.quantity_boxes) || 0)
    );
  }, 0);

  async function createOrder(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    if (!vendorId) {
      setMessage("Please select a vendor.");
      return;
    }

    if (
      items.length === 0 ||
      items.some(
        (item) =>
          !item.product_id ||
          !Number.isInteger(Number(item.quantity_boxes)) ||
          Number(item.quantity_boxes) < 1
      )
    ) {
      setMessage(
        "Select a product and enter a valid box quantity for every item."
      );
      return;
    }

    const duplicateProducts =
      items.some((item, index) =>
        items.some(
          (otherItem, otherIndex) =>
            index !== otherIndex &&
            String(item.product_id) ===
              String(otherItem.product_id)
        )
      );

    if (duplicateProducts) {
      setMessage(
        "Each product should appear only once. Combine its quantities into one row."
      );
      return;
    }

    const insufficientStock = items.some((item) => {
      const product = products.find(
        (productItem) =>
          String(getProductId(productItem)) ===
          String(item.product_id)
      );

      return (
        !product ||
        product.status !== "active" ||
        Number(item.quantity_boxes) >
          Number(product.quantity_available)
      );
    });

    if (insufficientStock) {
      setMessage(
        "One or more products are inactive or do not have enough boxes in stock."
      );
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);

    try {
      await parseResponse(
        await fetch(`${API}/orders`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
          },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            vendor_id: Number(vendorId),
            items: items.map((item) => ({
              product_id: Number(item.product_id),
              quantity_boxes: Number(item.quantity_boxes),
            })),
            notes,
          }),
        }),
        "Could not create order."
      );

      setMessage("Order created successfully!");
      resetOrderForm();
      setShowForm(false);
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setMessage(err.message || "Could not create order.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <main className="orders-page">
        <p>Loading orders...</p>
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (error && orders.length === 0) {
    return (
      <main className="orders-page">
        <p className="orders-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>

        <button type="button" onClick={loadData}>
          Try Again
        </button>
      </main>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main className="orders-page">
      <header className="orders-heading">
        <div>
          <h1>Orders Management</h1>
          <p>View and track fruit orders from  vendors.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="orders-count">
          <span>Total Orders</span>
          <strong>{orders.length}</strong>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && (
        <p className="orders-error" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="orders-actions">
        <button
          type="button"
          className="new-order-button"
          onClick={() => {
            setMessage("");
            setShowForm((current) => !current);
          }}
        >
          {showForm ? "Close Form" : "+ New Order"}
        </button>

        <button type="button" onClick={loadData}>
          Refresh
        </button>
      </div>

      {message && (
        <p className="orders-message" role="status">
          {message}
        </p>
      )}

      {showForm && (
        <form className="new-order-form" onSubmit={createOrder}>
          <h2>Create New Order</h2>

          <label htmlFor="vendor">Select Vendor *</label>
          <select
            id="vendor"
            value={vendorId}
            onChange={(event) => setVendorId(event.target.value)}
            required
          >
            <option value="">Choose a vendor</option>

            {vendors
              .filter((vendor) => vendor.status === "active")
              .map((vendor) => (
                <option key={vendor.id} value={vendor.id}>
                  {vendor.vendor_name} â€” {vendor.business_name}
                </option>
              ))}
          </select>

          <h3>Order Items</h3>

          {items.map((item, index) => (
            <div className="order-item-row" key={index}>
              <div className="order-field">
                <label htmlFor={`product-${index}`}>
                  Fruit Product *
                </label>

                <select
                  id={`product-${index}`}
                  value={item.product_id}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateItem(
                      index,
                      "product_id",
                      event.target.value
                    )
                  }
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Choose a product</option>

                  {products
                    .filter(
                      (product) =>
                        product.status === "active" &&
                        Number(product.quantity_available) > 0
                    )
                    .map((product) => (
                      <option
                        key={getProductId(product)}
                        value={getProductId(product)}
                      >
                        {getProductName(product)} (M{" "}
                        {Number(product.selling_price).toFixed(2)},
                        {" "}stock: {product.quantity_available})
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </div>

              <div className="order-field">
                <label htmlFor={`quantity-${index}`}>
                  Boxes *
                </label>

                <input
                  id={`quantity-${index}`}
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  step="1"
                  max={
                    products.find(
                      (product) =>
                        String(getProductId(product)) ===
                        String(item.product_id)
                    )?.quantity_available
                  }
                  value={item.quantity_boxes}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    updateItem(
                      index,
                      "quantity_boxes",
                      event.target.value
                    )
                  }
                  required
                />
              </div>

              {items.length > 1 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => removeItem(index)}
                >
                  Remove
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}

          <button type="button" onClick={addItem}>
            + Add Another Product
          </button>

          <label htmlFor="order-notes">Notes (optional)</label>
          <textarea
            id="order-notes"
            value={notes}
            onChange={(event) => setNotes(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Enter any special instructions"
            rows={3}
          />

          <div className="order-total">
            Estimated Total: M{" "}
            {total.toLocaleString("en-LS", {
              minimumFractionDigits: 2,
              maximumFractionDigits: 2,
            })}
          </div>

          <button type="submit" disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? "Creating Order..." : "Create Order"}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}

      {detailsLoading && <p>Loading order details...</p>}

      {selectedOrder && (
        <section className="order-details">
          <div className="order-details-heading">
            <div>
              <h2>Order Details</h2>
              <p>{selectedOrder.order_number}</p>
            </div>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setSelectedOrder(null)}
            >
              Close
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="order-details-info">
            <p>
              <strong>Vendor:</strong>{" "}
              {selectedOrder.vendor_name}
            </p>

            <p>
              <strong>Business:</strong>{" "}
              {selectedOrder.business_name}
            </p>

            <p>
              <strong>Date:</strong>{" "}
              {selectedOrder.order_date
                ? new Date(
                    selectedOrder.order_date
                  ).toLocaleDateString()
                : "â€”"}
            </p>

            <p>
              <strong>Status:</strong>{" "}
              {formatStatus(selectedOrder.status)}
            </p>

            <p>
              <strong>Total:</strong> M{" "}
              {Number(selectedOrder.total_amount).toFixed(2)}
            </p>

            <p>
              <strong>Notes:</strong>{" "}
              {selectedOrder.notes || "No notes"}
            </p>
          </div>

          {selectedOrder.status !== "completed" &&
            selectedOrder.status !== "cancelled" && (
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={cancellingId === selectedOrder.id}
                onClick={() => cancelOrder(selectedOrder)}
              >
                {cancellingId === selectedOrder.id
                  ? "Cancelling..."
                  : "Cancel This Order"}
              </button>
            )}

          <h3>Products Ordered</h3>

          <div className="orders-table-container">
            <table className="orders-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>Fruit</th>
                  <th>Box Size</th>
                  <th>Quantity</th>
                  <th>Unit Price</th>
                  <th>Subtotal</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {(selectedOrder.items || []).map(
                  (item, index) => (
                    <tr key={item.id || index}>
                      <td>{item.fruit}</td>
                      <td>{item.size}</td>
                      <td>{item.quantity_boxes}</td>
                      <td>
                        M {Number(item.unit_price).toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                      <td>
                        M {Number(item.subtotal).toFixed(2)}
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )
                )}

                {(!selectedOrder.items ||
                  selectedOrder.items.length === 0) && (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={5} className="orders-empty">
                      No order items were returned.
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      <div className="orders-table-container">
        <table className="orders-table">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th>Order Number</th>
              <th>Vendor</th>
              <th>Business</th>
              <th>Order Date</th>
              <th>Total Amount</th>
              <th>Status</th>
              <th>Update Status</th>
              <th>Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>

          <tbody>
            {orders.length === 0 ? (
              <tr>
                <td colSpan={8} className="orders-empty">
                  No orders found.
                </td>
              </tr>
            ) : (
              orders.map((order) => (
                <tr key={order.id}>
                  <td className="order-number">
                    {order.order_number}
                  </td>

                  <td>{order.vendor_name}</td>
                  <td>{order.business_name}</td>

                  <td>
                    {order.order_date
                      ? new Date(
                          order.order_date
                        ).toLocaleDateString()
                      : "â€”"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="order-amount">
                    M{" "}
                    {Number(order.total_amount).toLocaleString(
                      "en-LS",
                      {
                        minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                        maximumFractionDigits: 2,
                      }
                    )}
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <span
                      className={`order-status status-${order.status}`}
                    >
                      {formatStatus(order.status)}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <select
                      value={order.status}
                      onChange={(event) =>
                        updateOrderStatus(
                          order.id,
                          event.target.value
                        )
                      }
                      disabled={
                        updatingId === order.id ||
                        order.status === "completed" ||
                        order.status === "cancelled"
                      }
                      aria-label={`Update status for ${order.order_number}`}
                    >
                      {orderStatuses.map((statusOption) => (
                        <option
                          key={statusOption.code}
                          value={statusOption.code}
                        >
                          {statusOption.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <div className="orders-row-actions">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => viewOrderDetails(order.id)}
                      >
                        View
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="cancel-order-button"
                        onClick={() => cancelOrder(order)}
                        disabled={
                          cancellingId === order.id ||
                          order.status === "completed" ||
                          order.status === "cancelled"
                        }
                      >
                        {cancellingId === order.id
                          ? "Cancelling..."
                          : order.status === "cancelled"
                            ? "Cancelled"
                            : "Cancel Order"}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))
            )}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

=== Sidebar.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { NavLink } from "react-router-dom";

const API_URL = "http://localhost:5001/api";

function Sidebar() {
  const [menuItems, setMenuItems] = useState([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadMenu() {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/menu-items`);
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not load menu.");

        const data = await response.json();
        setMenuItems(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Could not load navigation menu:", error);
        setMenuItems([]);
      }
    }

    loadMenu();
  }, []);

  return (
    <header className="top-navigation">
      <div className="top-navigation-brand">
        <div className="brand-icon">BF</div>
        <div>
          <h2>Batloung</h2>
          <p>FRUITS DISTRIBUTION</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <nav className="top-navigation-links">
        {menuItems.map((item) => (
          <NavLink
            key={item.path}
            to={item.path}
            end={item.path === "/"}
            className={({ isActive }) =>
              isActive
                ? "top-navigation-link active"
                : "top-navigation-link"
            }
          >
            {item.name}
          </NavLink>
        ))}
      </nav>
    </header>
  );
}

export default Sidebar;

        <h3 className="stat-card-value">{value}</h3>

        {description && (
          <p className="stat-card-description">{description}</p>
