=== Dashboard.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import StatCard from "../components/StatCard";

const API_URL = "http://localhost:5001/api";

const money = (amount) =>
  `M ${Number(amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-LS", {
    minimumFractionDigits: 2,
    maximumFractionDigits: 2,
  })}`;

function Dashboard() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState(null);
  const [fruits, setFruits] = useState([]);
  const [orderStatuses, setOrderStatuses] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadDashboard() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const [statsResponse, fruitsResponse, statusesResponse] =
          await Promise.all([
            fetch(`${API_URL}/dashboard`),
            fetch(`${API_URL}/fruits`),
            fetch(`${API_URL}/order-statuses`),
          ]);

        if (
          !statsResponse.ok ||
          !fruitsResponse.ok ||
          !statusesResponse.ok
        ) {
          throw new Error("Could not load dashboard data.");
        }

        const [statsData, fruitsData, statusesData] = await Promise.all([
          statsResponse.json(),
          fruitsResponse.json(),
          statusesResponse.json(),
        ]);

        setStats(statsData);
        setFruits(Array.isArray(fruitsData) ? fruitsData : []);
        setOrderStatuses(Array.isArray(statusesData) ? statusesData : []);
      } catch (err) {
        setError(
          `${err.message} Make sure the backend is running on port 5001.`
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    loadDashboard();
  }, []);

  
const statistics = [
  { title: "Total Fruits", value: stats?.totalFruits ?? 0, icon: "ðŸŽ" },
  { title: "Total Suppliers", value: stats?.totalSuppliers ?? 0, icon: "ðŸšš" },
  { title: "Registered Vendors", value: stats?.registeredVendors ?? 0, icon: "ðŸ§‘â€ðŸ’¼" },
  { title: "Available Stock", value: stats?.availableStock ?? 0, icon: "ðŸ“¦" },
  { title: "Total Orders", value: stats?.totalOrders ?? 0, icon: "ðŸ›’" },
  { title: "Pending Orders", value: stats?.pendingOrders ?? 0, icon: "â³" },
  { title: "Completed Orders", value: stats?.completedOrders ?? 0, icon: "âœ…" },
  { title: "Sales Value", value: money(stats?.salesValue), icon: "ðŸ’°" },
];


  return (
    <div className="dashboard">
      <header className="dashboard-header">
        <div>
          <h1>Batloung Fruits</h1>
          <p>Distribution Management System</p>
        </div>
        <span className="admin-label">Administrator</span>
      </header>

      <section className="welcome-section">
        <h2>Dashboard Overview</h2>
        <p>Welcome! Here's an overview of  fruit distribution business.</p>
      </section>

      {error && <p className="form-error">{error}</p>}

      <section className="statistics-grid">
        {loading
          ? statistics.map((item) => (
              <StatCard
                key={item.title}
                title={item.title}
                value="..."
                icon={item.icon}
              />
            ))
          : statistics.map((item) => (
              <StatCard
                key={item.title}
                title={item.title}
                value={item.value}
                icon={item.icon}
              />
            ))}
      </section>

      <section className="dashboard-content">
        <div className="content-card">
          <h3>Fruit Inventory</h3>
          <p>Manage  fruits, box sizes and available stock.</p>
          <ul>
            {loading ? (
              <li>Loading fruits...</li>
            ) : fruits.length ? (
              fruits.map((fruit) => <li key={fruit.id}>{fruit.fruit_name}</li>)
            ) : (
              <li>No fruits found.</li>
            )}
          </ul>
        </div>

        <div className="content-card">
          <h3>Order Management</h3>
          <p>Monitor street vendor orders and their progress.</p>
          <ul>
            {loading ? (
              <li>Loading statuses...</li>
            ) : orderStatuses.length ? (
              orderStatuses.map((status) => (
                <li key={status.id}>{status.label}</li>
              ))
            ) : (
              <li>No order statuses found.</li>
            )}
          </ul>
        </div>
      </section>

      <footer className="dashboard-footer">
        Batloung Fruits Distribution Management System
      </footer>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Dashboard;


=== Fruits.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API_URL = "http://localhost:5001/api";

const emptyFruitForm = {
  fruit_name: "",
  description: "",
  status: "active",
};

const emptyProductForm = {
  fruit_id: "",
  box_size_id: "",
  quantity_per_box: "10",
  selling_price: "",
  quantity_available: "0",
  status: "active",
};

async function readResponse(response, fallback) {
  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(data.message || fallback);
  }

  return data;
}

function Fruits() {
  const [fruits, setFruits] = useState([]);
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [boxSizes, setBoxSizes] = useState([]);

  const [fruitForm, setFruitForm] = useState(emptyFruitForm);
  const [productForm, setProductForm] = useState(emptyProductForm);

  const [editingFruitId, setEditingFruitId] = useState(null);
  const [editingProductId, setEditingProductId] = useState(null);

  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [savingFruit, setSavingFruit] = useState(false);
  const [savingProduct, setSavingProduct] = useState(false);

  async function loadData() {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const [fruitResponse, sizeResponse, productResponse] =
        await Promise.all([
          fetch(`${API_URL}/fruits`),
          fetch(`${API_URL}/box-sizes`),
          fetch(`${API_URL}/products`),
        ]);

      const [fruitData, sizeData, productData] = await Promise.all([
        readResponse(fruitResponse, "Failed to load fruits."),
        readResponse(sizeResponse, "Failed to load box sizes."),
        readResponse(productResponse, "Failed to load products."),
      ]);

      setFruits(Array.isArray(fruitData) ? fruitData : []);
      setBoxSizes(Array.isArray(sizeData) ? sizeData : []);
      setProducts(Array.isArray(productData) ? productData : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        `${err.message} Make sure the backend is running on port 5001 and the API routes exist.`
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  async function saveFruit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    setSavingFruit(true);

    try {
      const url = editingFruitId
        ? `${API_URL}/fruits/${editingFruitId}`
        : `${API_URL}/fruits`;

      const result = await readResponse(
        await fetch(url, {
          method: editingFruitId ? "PUT" : "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(fruitForm),
        }),
        "Could not save fruit."
      );

      setNotice(
        result.message ||
          (editingFruitId
            ? "Fruit updated successfully."
            : "Fruit added successfully.")
      );

      setFruitForm(emptyFruitForm);
      setEditingFruitId(null);
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSavingFruit(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveProduct(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setNotice("");

    if (!productForm.fruit_id || !productForm.box_size_id) {
      setError("Please select a fruit and box size.");
      return;
    }

    const price = Number(productForm.selling_price);
    const perBox = Number(productForm.quantity_per_box);
    const stock = Number(productForm.quantity_available);

    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0) {
      setError("Selling price must be greater than zero.");
      return;
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(perBox) || perBox <= 0) {
      setError("Quantity per box must be a positive whole number.");
      return;
    }

    if (!Number.isInteger(stock) || stock < 0) {
      setError("Stock must be zero or a positive whole number.");
      return;
    }

    setSavingProduct(true);

    try {
      const url = editingProductId
        ? `${API_URL}/products/${editingProductId}`
        : `${API_URL}/products`;

      const result = await readResponse(
        await fetch(url, {
          method: editingProductId ? "PUT" : "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            fruit_id: Number(productForm.fruit_id),
            box_size_id: Number(productForm.box_size_id),
            quantity_per_box: perBox,
            selling_price: price,
            quantity_available: stock,
            status: productForm.status,
          }),
        }),
        "Could not save product."
      );

      setNotice(
        result.message ||
          (editingProductId
            ? "Product updated successfully."
            : "Product added successfully.")
      );

      setProductForm(emptyProductForm);
      setEditingProductId(null);
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSavingProduct(false);
    }
  }

  function editFruit(fruit) {
    setEditingFruitId(fruit.id);
    setFruitForm({
      fruit_name: fruit.fruit_name || "",
      description: fruit.description || "",
      status: fruit.status || "active",
    });
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  }

  function editProduct(product) {
    setEditingProductId(product.id);
    setProductForm({
      fruit_id: String(product.fruit_id ?? ""),
      box_size_id: String(product.box_size_id ?? ""),
      quantity_per_box: String(product.quantity_per_box ?? ""),
      selling_price: String(product.selling_price ?? ""),
      quantity_available: String(product.quantity_available ?? "0"),
      status: product.status || "active",
    });
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: "smooth" });
  }

  async function deleteRecord(type, record) {
    const isFruit = type === "fruit";
    const label = isFruit
      ? record.fruit_name
      : `${record.fruit_name || "Product"} - ${record.size_name || "Box"}`;

    if (
      !window.confirm(
        `Delete ${label}? This action may be blocked if other records depend on it.`
      )
    ) {
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setNotice("");

    try {
      const result = await readResponse(
        await fetch(
          `${API_URL}/${isFruit ? "fruits" : "products"}/${record.id}`,
          { method: "DELETE" }
        ),
        `Could not delete ${type}.`
      );

      setNotice(
        result.message || `${isFruit ? "Fruit" : "Product"} deleted successfully.`
      );

      if (isFruit && editingFruitId === record.id) {
        setEditingFruitId(null);
        setFruitForm(emptyFruitForm);
      }

      if (!isFruit && editingProductId === record.id) {
        setEditingProductId(null);
        setProductForm(emptyProductForm);
      }

      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    }
  }

  function cancelFruitEdit() {
    setEditingFruitId(null);
    setFruitForm(emptyFruitForm);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
  }

  function cancelProductEdit() {
    setEditingProductId(null);
    setProductForm(emptyProductForm);
    setError("");
    setNotice("");
  }

  return (
    <div className="fruits-page">
      <div className="page-heading">
        <div>
          <h1>Fruit Management</h1>
          <p>Manage fruit types, products, box sizes and stock.</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="form-error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
      {notice && <p role="status">{notice}</p>}

      <section className="management-section">
        <form className="fruit-form product-form" onSubmit={saveFruit}>
          <h2>{editingFruitId ? "Edit Fruit Type" : "Add Fruit Type"}</h2>

          <div className="product-form-grid">
            <label>
              Fruit Name
              <input
                value={fruitForm.fruit_name}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setFruitForm({ ...fruitForm, fruit_name: event.target.value })
                }
                maxLength="60"
                placeholder="Please enter fruit name"
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              Description
              <input
                value={fruitForm.description}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setFruitForm({ ...fruitForm, description: event.target.value })
                }
                maxLength="255"
                placeholder="Optional description"
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              Status
              <select
                value={fruitForm.status}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setFruitForm({ ...fruitForm, status: event.target.value })
                }
              >
                <option value="active">Active</option>
                <option value="inactive">Inactive</option>
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="form-actions">
            <button className="primary-button" type="submit" disabled={savingFruit}>
              {savingFruit ? "Saving..." : editingFruitId ? "Update Fruit" : "Save Fruit Type"}
            </button>
            {editingFruitId && (
              <button type="button" onClick={cancelFruitEdit}>Cancel</button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="management-section">
        <h2>Fruit Types</h2>
        {loading ? <p>Loading fruits...</p> : (
          <div className="table-container">
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>ID</th>
                  <th>Fruit Name</th>
                  <th>Description</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {fruits.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr><td colSpan="5">No fruit records found.</td></tr>
                ) : fruits.map((fruit) => (
                  <tr key={fruit.id}>
                    <td>{fruit.id}</td>
                    <td>{fruit.fruit_name}</td>
                    <td>{fruit.description || "â€”"}</td>
                    <td>{fruit.status || "â€”"}</td>
                    <td>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => editFruit(fruit)}>Edit</button>{" "}
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => deleteRecord("fruit", fruit)}>Delete</button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      <section className="management-section">
        <form className="fruit-form product-form" onSubmit={saveProduct}>
          <h2>{editingProductId ? "Edit Fruit Product" : "Add Fruit Product"}</h2>

          <div className="product-form-grid">
            <label>
              Fruit
              <select
                value={productForm.fruit_id}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setProductForm({ ...productForm, fruit_id: event.target.value })
                }
                required
              >
                <option value="">Select fruit</option>
                {fruits.filter((fruit) => fruit.status !== "inactive").map((fruit) => (
                  <option key={fruit.id} value={fruit.id}>{fruit.fruit_name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>

            <label>
              Box Size
              <select
                value={productForm.box_size_id}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setProductForm({ ...productForm, box_size_id: event.target.value })
                }
                required
              >
                <option value="">Select box size</option>
                {boxSizes.map((size) => (
                  <option key={size.id} value={size.id}>{size.size_name}</option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>

            <label>
              Quantity per Box
              <input
                type="number"
                min="1"
                step="1"
                value={productForm.quantity_per_box}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setProductForm({ ...productForm, quantity_per_box: event.target.value })
                }
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              Selling Price (M)
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0.01"
                step="0.01"
                value={productForm.selling_price}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setProductForm({ ...productForm, selling_price: event.target.value })
                }
                placeholder="Please enter selling price"
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              Quantity in Stock (boxes)
              <input
                type="number"
                min="0"
                step="1"
                value={productForm.quantity_available}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setProductForm({ ...productForm, quantity_available: event.target.value })
                }
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label>
              Status
              <select
                value={productForm.status}
                onChange={(event) =>
                  setProductForm({ ...productForm, status: event.target.value })
                }
              >
                <option value="active">Active</option>
                <option value="inactive">Inactive</option>
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>

          {loading && <p>Loading form options...</p>}
          {!loading && fruits.length === 0 && <p>Save a fruit type first to select it here.</p>}
          {!loading && boxSizes.length === 0 && <p>No box sizes are available yet.</p>}

          <div className="form-actions">
            <button
              className="primary-button"
              type="submit"
              disabled={savingProduct || loading || fruits.length === 0 || boxSizes.length === 0}
            >
              {savingProduct ? "Saving..." : editingProductId ? "Update Product" : "Save Product"}
            </button>
            {editingProductId && (
              <button type="button" onClick={cancelProductEdit}>Cancel</button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="management-section">
        <h2>Fruit Products and Stock</h2>
        {loading ? <p>Loading products...</p> : (
          <div className="table-container">
            <table>
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>ID</th>
                  <th>Fruit</th>
                  <th>Box Size</th>
                  <th>Quantity per Box</th>
                  <th>Selling Price</th>
                  <th>Available Boxes</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {products.length === 0 ? (
                  <tr><td colSpan="8">No product records found.</td></tr>
                ) : products.map((product) => (
                  <tr key={product.id}>
                    <td>{product.id}</td>
                    <td>{product.fruit_name || `Fruit ID ${product.fruit_id}`}</td>
                    <td>{product.size_name || `Box ID ${product.box_size_id}`}</td>
                    <td>{product.quantity_per_box}</td>
                    <td>M {Number(product.selling_price || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
                    <td>{product.quantity_available}</td>
                    <td>{product.status}</td>
                    <td>
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => editProduct(product)}>Edit</button>{" "}
                      <button type="button" onClick={() => deleteRecord("product", product)}>Delete</button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Fruits;


=== Reports.jsx ===
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

const API = "http://localhost:5001/api";

function Reports() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([]);
  const [fruitStats, setFruitStats] = useState([]);
  const [orderStatuses, setOrderStatuses] = useState([]);
  const [period, setPeriod] = useState("all");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadReports() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");

      try {
        const statusesResponse = await fetch(`${API}/order-statuses`);
        if (statusesResponse.ok) {
          const statusesData = await statusesResponse.json();
          setOrderStatuses(
            Array.isArray(statusesData) ? statusesData : []
          );
        }

        const response = await fetch(`${API}/orders`);
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error("Could not load orders.");

        const data = await response.json();
        setOrders(data);

        const details = await Promise.all(
          data.map(async (order) => {
            try {
              const result = await fetch(`${API}/orders/${order.id}`);
              if (!result.ok) return null;
              return await result.json();
            } catch {
              return null;
            }
          })
        );

        const map = {};

        details.forEach((order) => {
          if (!order || order.status === "cancelled") return;

          (order.items || []).forEach((item) => {
            const fruit = item.fruit || item.fruit_name || "Unknown fruit";
            const size = item.size || item.size_name || "";
            const key = `${fruit}__${size}`;

            if (!map[key]) {
              map[key] = { fruit, size, boxes: 0, sales: 0 };
            }

            map[key].boxes += Number(item.quantity_boxes) || 0;

            if (order.status === "completed") {
              map[key].sales += Number(item.subtotal) || 0;
            }
          });
        });

        setFruitStats(Object.values(map));
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err.message || "Could not load reports.");
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    loadReports();
  }, []);

  const filteredOrders = useMemo(() => {
    const now = new Date();

    return orders.filter((order) => {
      if (period === "all") return true;

      const date = new Date(order.order_date);
      if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return false;

      if (period === "today") {
        return date.toDateString() === now.toDateString();
      }

      const days = Number(period);
      const start = new Date(now);
      start.setDate(now.getDate() - days + 1);
      start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);

      return date >= start && date <= now;
    });
  }, [orders, period]);

  const completed = filteredOrders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "completed"
  );
  const pending = filteredOrders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "pending"
  );
  const cancelled = filteredOrders.filter(
    (order) => order.status === "cancelled"
  );

  const revenue = completed.reduce(
    (sum, order) => sum + (Number(order.total_amount) || 0),
    0
  );

  const money = (amount) =>
    `M ${Number(amount || 0).toLocaleString("en-LS", {
      minimumFractionDigits: 2,
      maximumFractionDigits: 2,
    })}`;

  const popular = useMemo(() => {
    return fruitStats
      .map((fruit) => ({ ...fruit }))
      .sort((a, b) => b.boxes - a.boxes);
  }, [fruitStats]);

  if (loading) {
    return <div className="reports-page"><p>Loading reports...</p></div>;
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <div className="reports-page">
        <h1>Sales and Reports</h1>
        <p className="orders-error">{error}</p>
        <button onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>Try Again</button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="reports-page">
      <div className="reports-heading">
        <div>
          <h1>Sales and Reports</h1>
          <p>Review sales performance and order activity.</p>
        </div>

        <label className="reports-period">
          Report period
          <select value={period} onChange={(e) => setPeriod(e.target.value)}>
            <option value="all">All time</option>
            <option value="today">Today</option>
            <option value="7">Last 7 days</option>
            <option value="30">Last 30 days</option>
          </select>
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="reports-cards">
        <div className="report-card">
          <span>Sales Revenue</span>
          <strong>{money(revenue)}</strong>
          <small>Completed orders only</small>
        </div>
        <div className="report-card">
          <span>Total Orders</span>
          <strong>{filteredOrders.length}</strong>
          <small>All statuses</small>
        </div>
        <div className="report-card">
          <span>Completed Orders</span>
          <strong>{completed.length}</strong>
          <small>Successfully completed</small>
        </div>
        <div className="report-card">
          <span>Pending Orders</span>
          <strong>{pending.length}</strong>
          <small>Awaiting confirmation</small>
        </div>
        <div className="report-card">
          <span>Cancelled Orders</span>
          <strong>{cancelled.length}</strong>
          <small>Excluded from revenue</small>
        </div>
      </div>

      <section className="reports-section">
        <h2>Order Status Summary</h2>
        <div className="reports-table-container">
          <table className="reports-table">
            <thead>
              <tr><th>Status</th><th>Number of Orders</th></tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {orderStatuses.map((statusOption) => (
                <tr key={statusOption.code}>
                  <td>{statusOption.label}</td>
                  <td>
                    {filteredOrders.filter(
                      (order) => order.status === statusOption.code
                    ).length}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="reports-section">
        <h2>Popular Fruits</h2>
        <p className="reports-description">
          Ranked by boxes ordered. Cancelled orders are excluded.
        </p>
        <div className="reports-table-container">
          <table className="reports-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Rank</th><th>Fruit</th><th>Box Size</th>
                <th>Boxes Ordered</th><th>Completed Sales</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {popular.length ? popular.map((fruit, index) => (
                <tr key={`${fruit.fruit}-${fruit.size}`}>
                  <td>{index + 1}</td>
                  <td>{fruit.fruit}</td>
                  <td>{fruit.size || "—"}</td>
                  <td>{fruit.boxes}</td>
                  <td>{money(fruit.sales)}</td>
                </tr>
              )) : (
                <tr><td colSpan="5">No fruit order details available.</td></tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="reports-section">
        <h2>Completed Orders Sales</h2>
        <div className="reports-table-container">
          <table className="reports-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Order Number</th><th>Date</th>
                <th>Vendor</th><th>Sales Amount</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {completed.length ? completed.map((order) => (
                <tr key={order.id}>
                  <td>{order.order_number}</td>
                  <td>{new Date(order.order_date).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                  <td>{order.vendor_name || "—"}</td>
                  <td>{money(order.total_amount)}</td>
                </tr>
              )) : (
                <tr><td colSpan="4">No completed orders in this period.</td></tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export default Reports;


=== Stock.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API = "http://localhost:5001/api";

const today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

const emptyPurchase = {
  supplier_id: "",
  product_id: "",
  quantity_boxes: "1",
  unit_cost: "",
  purchase_date: today(),
  notes: "",
};

async function parseResponse(response, fallback) {
  const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(data.message || fallback);
  }

  return data;
}

export default function Stock() {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([]);
  const [suppliers, setSuppliers] = useState([]);

  const [purchaseForm, setPurchaseForm] = useState(emptyPurchase);
  const [stockForm, setStockForm] = useState({
    product_id: "",
    quantity: "",
  });

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [savingPurchase, setSavingPurchase] = useState(false);
  const [savingStock, setSavingStock] = useState(false);
  const [purchases, setPurchases] = useState([]);
  const [loadingPurchases, setLoadingPurchases] = useState(true);

  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");

  async function loadPurchases() {
    setLoadingPurchases(true);
    try {
      const resp = await fetch(`${API}/purchases`);
      const data = await resp.json().catch(() => []);
      if (!resp.ok) throw new Error(data.message || "Could not load purchases.");
      setPurchases(Array.isArray(data) ? data : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setLoadingPurchases(false);
    }
  }

  async function loadData() {
    setLoading(true);
    setError("");

    try {
      const responses = await Promise.all([
        fetch(`${API}/stock`),
        fetch(`${API}/suppliers`),
      ]);

      const [stockData, supplierData] = await Promise.all(
        responses.map((response) =>
          parseResponse(response, "Could not load stock or suppliers.")
        )
      );

      const loadedProducts = Array.isArray(stockData) ? stockData : [];
      const loadedSuppliers = Array.isArray(supplierData)
        ? supplierData
        : [];

      setProducts(loadedProducts);
      setSuppliers(loadedSuppliers);

      setPurchaseForm((current) => ({
        ...current,
        supplier_id:
          current.supplier_id ||
          (loadedSuppliers[0] ? String(loadedSuppliers[0].id) : ""),
        product_id:
          current.product_id ||
          (loadedProducts[0]
            ? String(loadedProducts[0].product_id)
            : ""),
      }));

      setStockForm((current) => {
        const selectedProduct = loadedProducts.find(
          (product) =>
            String(product.product_id) === current.product_id
        );

        return {
          product_id: selectedProduct
            ? current.product_id
            : loadedProducts[0]
              ? String(loadedProducts[0].product_id)
              : "",
          quantity: selectedProduct
            ? current.quantity
            : loadedProducts[0]
              ? String(loadedProducts[0].quantity_available)
              : "",
        };
      });
      await loadPurchases();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        `${err.message} Make sure the backend is running on port 5001.`
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function handlePurchaseChange(event) {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setPurchaseForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  }

  function handleStockProductChange(event) {
    const productId = event.target.value;

    const selected = products.find(
      (product) => String(product.product_id) === productId
    );

    setStockForm({
      product_id: productId,
      quantity: selected
        ? String(selected.quantity_available)
        : "",
    });
  }

  async function savePurchase(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setNotice("");

    const boxes = Number(purchaseForm.quantity_boxes);
    const cost = Number(purchaseForm.unit_cost);

    if (
      !purchaseForm.supplier_id ||
      !purchaseForm.product_id ||
      !Number.isInteger(boxes) ||
      boxes <= 0 ||
      purchaseForm.unit_cost.trim() === "" ||
      !Number.isFinite(cost) ||
      cost < 0 ||
      !purchaseForm.purchase_date
    ) {
      setError(
        "Select a supplier and product, enter a valid quantity and cost, and choose a purchase date."
      );
      return;
    }

    setSavingPurchase(true);

    try {
      const result = await parseResponse(
        await fetch(`${API}/purchases`, {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            ...purchaseForm,
            supplier_id: Number(purchaseForm.supplier_id),
            product_id: Number(purchaseForm.product_id),
            quantity_boxes: boxes,
            unit_cost: cost,
          }),
        }),
        "Could not record purchase."
      );

      setNotice(
        `${result.message || "Purchase recorded successfully."} Total cost: M ${Number(
          result.total_cost ?? boxes * cost
        ).toFixed(2)}.`
      );

      setPurchaseForm({
        ...emptyPurchase,
        supplier_id: suppliers[0] ? String(suppliers[0].id) : "",
        product_id: products[0]
          ? String(products[0].product_id)
          : "",
      });

      await loadData();
      await loadPurchases();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSavingPurchase(false);
    }
  }

  async function saveStock(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setNotice("");

    const quantity = Number(stockForm.quantity);

    if (
      !stockForm.product_id ||
      stockForm.quantity.trim() === "" ||
      !Number.isInteger(quantity) ||
      quantity < 0
    ) {
      setError(
        "Choose a product and enter a whole-number stock quantity of zero or more."
      );
      return;
    }

    setSavingStock(true);

    try {
      const result = await parseResponse(
        await fetch(`${API}/stock/${stockForm.product_id}`, {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            quantity_available: quantity,
          }),
        }),
        "Could not update stock."
      );

      setNotice(result.message || "Stock quantity updated successfully.");
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setSavingStock(false);
    }
  }

  const totalBoxes = products.reduce(
    (total, product) =>
      total + Number(product.quantity_available || 0),
    0
  );

  const selectedProduct = products.find(
    (product) =>
      String(product.product_id) === stockForm.product_id
  );

  const purchaseTotal =
    Math.max(0, Number(purchaseForm.quantity_boxes) || 0) *
    Math.max(0, Number(purchaseForm.unit_cost) || 0);

  return (
    <main className="management-page">
      <header className="page-header">
        <div>
          <h1>Stock Management</h1>
          <p>Record fruit purchases and manage available stock.</p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {error && (
        <p className="error-message" role="alert">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}

      {notice && (
        <p className="success-message" role="status">
          {notice}
        </p>
      )}

      <div className="management-toolbar">
        <span>Products: {products.length}</span>
        <span>Total boxes in stock: {totalBoxes}</span>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <p>Loading stock and supplier information...</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <form className="management-form" onSubmit={savePurchase}>
            <h2>Record New Purchase</h2>
            <p>
              Record boxes purchased from a supplier.  backend should
              update available stock when a purchase is saved.
            </p>

            <div className="product-form-grid">
              <label>
                Supplier *
                <select
                  name="supplier_id"
                  value={purchaseForm.supplier_id}
                  onChange={handlePurchaseChange}
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Select supplier</option>
                  {suppliers.map((supplier) => (
                    <option key={supplier.id} value={supplier.id}>
                      {supplier.supplier_name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>

              <label>
                Fruit Product / Box Size *
                <select
                  name="product_id"
                  value={purchaseForm.product_id}
                  onChange={handlePurchaseChange}
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Select product</option>
                  {products
                    .filter((product) => product.status === "active")
                    .map((product) => (
                      <option
                        key={product.product_id}
                        value={product.product_id}
                      >
                        {product.fruit} â€” {product.box_size} (
                        {product.quantity_available} boxes available)
                      </option>
                    ))}
                </select>
              </label>

              <label>
                Quantity Purchased (boxes) *
                <input
                  name="quantity_boxes"
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  step="1"
                  value={purchaseForm.quantity_boxes}
                  onChange={handlePurchaseChange}
                  required
                />
              </label>

              <label>
                Cost per Box (M) *
                <input
                  name="unit_cost"
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="0.01"
                  value={purchaseForm.unit_cost}
                  onChange={handlePurchaseChange}
                  placeholder="Please enter cost per box"
                  required
                />
              </label>

              <label>
                Purchase Date *
                <input
                  name="purchase_date"
                  type="date"
                  value={purchaseForm.purchase_date}
                  onChange={handlePurchaseChange}
                  required
                />
              </label>

              <label>
                Notes (optional)
                <input
                  name="notes"
                  type="text"
                  maxLength="255"
                  value={purchaseForm.notes}
                  onChange={handlePurchaseChange}
                  placeholder="Please enter notes"
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            <p className="purchase-total">
              <strong>Total Purchase Cost:</strong>{" "}
              M{" "}
              {purchaseTotal.toLocaleString("en-LS", {
                minimumFractionDigits: 2,
                maximumFractionDigits: 2,
              })}
            </p>

            <div className="form-actions">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={
                  savingPurchase ||
                  suppliers.length === 0 ||
                  !products.some((product) => product.status === "active")
                }
              >
                {savingPurchase ? "Saving Purchase..." : "Save Purchase"}
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  setPurchaseForm({
                    ...emptyPurchase,
                    supplier_id: suppliers[0]
                      ? String(suppliers[0].id)
                      : "",
                    product_id: products[0]
                      ? String(products[0].product_id)
                      : "",
                  });
                  setError("");
                  setNotice("");
                }}
              >
                Clear Form
              </button>
            </div>

            {suppliers.length === 0 && (
              <p>Add a supplier before recording a purchase.</p>
            )}

            {!products.some(
              (product) => product.status === "active"
            ) && (
              <p>
                An active product is required before recording a purchase.
              </p>
            )}
          </form>

          <form className="management-form" onSubmit={saveStock}>
            <h2>Update Available Stock</h2>
            <p>
              Set the current number of boxes available for a product.
            </p>

            <div className="product-form-grid">
              <label>
                Select Fruit Product *
                <select
                  value={stockForm.product_id}
                  onChange={handleStockProductChange}
                  required
                >
                  <option value="">Select product</option>
                  {products.map((product) => (
                    <option
                      key={product.product_id}
                      value={product.product_id}
                    >
                      {product.fruit} â€” {product.box_size}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>

              <label>
                Available Boxes *
                <input
                  type="number"
                  min="0"
                  step="1"
                  value={stockForm.quantity}
                  onChange={(event) =>
                    setStockForm((current) => ({
                      ...current,
                      quantity: event.target.value,
                    }))
                  }
                  required
                />
              </label>
            </div>

            {selectedProduct && (
              <p>
                Current saved stock:{" "}
                <strong>{selectedProduct.quantity_available} boxes</strong>
              </p>
            )}

            <div className="form-actions">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={savingStock || products.length === 0}
              >
                {savingStock ? "Updating Stock..." : "Save Stock"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>

          <section className="management-section">
            <h2>Purchases</h2>
            {loadingPurchases ? (
              <p>Loading purchases...</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="table-container">
                <table>
                  <thead>
                    <tr>
                      <th>ID</th>
                      <th>Supplier</th>
                      <th>Fruit</th>
                      <th>Box Size</th>
                      <th>Quantity</th>
                      <th>Unit Cost</th>
                      <th>Total Cost</th>
                      <th>Purchase Date</th>
                      <th>Notes</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {purchases.length === 0 ? (
                      <tr>
                        <td colSpan="9">No purchases recorded yet.</td>
                      </tr>
                    ) : (
                      purchases.map((purchase) => (
                        <tr key={purchase.id}>
                          <td>{purchase.id}</td>
                          <td>{purchase.supplier_name}</td>
                          <td>{purchase.fruit}</td>
                          <td>{purchase.box_size}</td>
                          <td>{purchase.quantity_boxes}</td>
                          <td>M {Number(purchase.unit_cost || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
                          <td>M {Number(purchase.total_cost || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
                          <td>{purchase.purchase_date ? new Date(purchase.purchase_date).toISOString().split("T")[0] : ""}</td>
                          <td>{purchase.notes || "-"}</td>
                        </tr>
                      ))
                    )}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="management-section">
            <h2>Stock Summary</h2>
            <div className="table-container">
              <table>
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th>ID</th>
                    <th>Fruit</th>
                    <th>Box Size</th>
                    <th>Qty/Box</th>
                    <th>Selling Price</th>
                    <th>Available Boxes</th>
                    <th>Status</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {products.map((product) => (
                    <tr key={product.product_id}>
                      <td>{product.product_id}</td>
                      <td>{product.fruit}</td>
                      <td>{product.box_size}</td>
                      <td>{product.quantity_per_box}</td>
                      <td>M {Number(product.selling_price || 0).toFixed(2)}</td>
                      <td>{product.quantity_available}</td>
                      <td>{product.status}</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </main>
  );
}


=== Suppliers.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API_URL = "http://localhost:5001/api";

const emptyForm = {
  supplier_name: "",
  province_id: "",
  contact_person: "",
  telephone: "",
  email: "",
  physical_address: "",
};

export default function Suppliers() {
  const [provinces, setProvinces] = useState([]);
  const [suppliers, setSuppliers] = useState([]);
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);
  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState(null);

  async function readResponse(response) {
    const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));

    if (!response.ok) {
      throw new Error(
        result.message || result.error || "The request failed."
      );
    }

    return result;
  }

  async function loadData() {
    setError("");
    setDataLoading(true);

    try {
      const [provinceResponse, supplierResponse] = await Promise.all([
        fetch(`${API_URL}/provinces`),
        fetch(`${API_URL}/suppliers`),
      ]);

      const [provinceData, supplierData] = await Promise.all([
        readResponse(provinceResponse),
        readResponse(supplierResponse),
      ]);

      setProvinces(
        Array.isArray(provinceData) ? provinceData : []
      );

      setSuppliers(
        Array.isArray(supplierData) ? supplierData : []
      );
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Could not load suppliers.");
    } finally {
      setDataLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadData();
  }, []);

  function handleChange(event) {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setForm((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  }

  function resetForm() {
    setForm({ ...emptyForm });
    setEditingId(null);
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const url = editingId
        ? `${API_URL}/suppliers/${editingId}`
        : `${API_URL}/suppliers`;

      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method: editingId ? "PUT" : "POST",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          ...form,
          province_id: Number(form.province_id),
        }),
      });

      const result = await readResponse(response);

      setMessage(
        result.message ||
          (editingId
            ? "Supplier updated successfully."
            : "Supplier saved successfully.")
      );

      resetForm();
      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Could not save supplier.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function handleEdit(supplier) {
    setEditingId(supplier.id);

    setForm({
      supplier_name: supplier.supplier_name || "",
      province_id: String(supplier.province_id || ""),
      contact_person: supplier.contact_person || "",
      telephone: supplier.telephone || "",
      email: supplier.email || "",
      physical_address: supplier.physical_address || "",
    });

    setMessage("");
    setError("");

    window.scrollTo({
      top: 0,
      behavior: "smooth",
    });
  }

  async function handleDelete(supplier) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      `Delete supplier "${supplier.supplier_name}"? This may be blocked if the supplier has related purchase records.`
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    setMessage("");
    setError("");
    setDeletingId(supplier.id);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(
        `${API_URL}/suppliers/${supplier.id}`,
        { method: "DELETE" }
      );

      const result = await readResponse(response);

      setMessage(
        result.message || "Supplier deleted successfully."
      );

      if (editingId === supplier.id) {
        resetForm();
      }

      await loadData();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.message ||
          "Could not delete supplier. Check whether it has related purchase records."
      );
    } finally {
      setDeletingId(null);
    }
  }

  function getProvinceName(supplier) {
    if (supplier.province_name) {
      return supplier.province_name;
    }

    const province = provinces.find(
      (item) =>
        Number(item.id) === Number(supplier.province_id)
    );

    return province?.name || "Not specified";
  }

  return (
    <main className="suppliers-page management-page">
      <header className="suppliers-header page-header">
        <div>
          <h1>Supplier Management</h1>
          <p>
            Register, view, update and manage fruit suppliers.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="suppliers-count">
          <span>Total Suppliers</span>
          <strong>{suppliers.length}</strong>
        </div>
      </header>

      {message && (
        <div className="supplier-message success-message" role="status">
          {message}
        </div>
      )}

      {error && (
        <div className="supplier-error error-message" role="alert">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      <section className="supplier-panel management-form">
        <h2>
          {editingId ? "Edit Supplier" : "Supplier Registration"}
        </h2>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="supplier-form-grid product-form-grid">
            <label className="supplier-field">
              Supplier Name *
              <input
                name="supplier_name"
                value={form.supplier_name}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter supplier name"
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label className="supplier-field">
              Province *
              <select
                name="province_id"
                value={form.province_id}
                onChange={handleChange}
                required
              >
                <option value="">Select a province</option>

                {provinces.map((province) => (
                  <option key={province.id} value={province.id}>
                    {province.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>

            <label className="supplier-field">
              Contact Person
              <input
                name="contact_person"
                value={form.contact_person}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter contact person"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="supplier-field">
              Telephone Number *
              <input
                name="telephone"
                type="tel"
                value={form.telephone}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter telephone number"
                required
              />
            </label>

            <label className="supplier-field">
              Email Address
              <input
                name="email"
                type="email"
                value={form.email}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter email address"
              />
            </label>

            <label className="supplier-field">
              Physical Address
              <input
                name="physical_address"
                value={form.physical_address}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter physical address"
              />
            </label>
          </div>

          {provinces.length === 0 && (
            <p className="supplier-warning">
              No provinces are available. Please check  provinces
              table and API.
            </p>
          )}

          <div className="supplier-form-actions form-actions">
            <button
              className="supplier-button supplier-button-primary"
              type="submit"
              disabled={loading || provinces.length === 0}
            >
              {loading
                ? "Saving..."
                : editingId
                  ? "Update Supplier"
                  : "Save Supplier"}
            </button>

            {editingId && (
              <button
                className="supplier-button supplier-button-secondary"
                type="button"
                onClick={resetForm}
                disabled={loading}
              >
                Cancel Edit
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="supplier-panel">
        <div className="supplier-table-heading">
          <div>
            <h2>Registered Suppliers</h2>
            <p>Total suppliers: {suppliers.length}</p>
          </div>

          <button
            className="supplier-button supplier-button-secondary"
            type="button"
            onClick={loadData}
            disabled={dataLoading}
          >
            {dataLoading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </div>

        {dataLoading && suppliers.length === 0 ? (
          <p>Loading suppliers...</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="supplier-table-wrapper orders-table-container">
            <table className="supplier-table orders-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>ID</th>
                  <th>Supplier Name</th>
                  <th>Province</th>
                  <th>Contact Person</th>
                  <th>Telephone</th>
                  <th>Email</th>
                  <th>Physical Address</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>

              <tbody>
                {suppliers.map((supplier) => (
                  <tr key={supplier.id}>
                    <td>{supplier.id}</td>
                    <td>{supplier.supplier_name}</td>
                    <td>{getProvinceName(supplier)}</td>
                    <td>{supplier.contact_person || "â€”"}</td>
                    <td>{supplier.telephone || "â€”"}</td>
                    <td>{supplier.email || "â€”"}</td>
                    <td>{supplier.physical_address || "â€”"}</td>
                    <td>
                      <div className="supplier-row-actions orders-row-actions">
                        <button
                          className="supplier-button supplier-button-edit"
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleEdit(supplier)}
                        >
                          Edit
                        </button>

                        <button
                          className="supplier-button supplier-button-delete cancel-order-button"
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleDelete(supplier)}
                          disabled={deletingId === supplier.id}
                        >
                          {deletingId === supplier.id
                            ? "Deleting..."
                            : "Delete"}
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}

                {suppliers.length === 0 && (
                  <tr>
                    <td colSpan={8} className="supplier-empty orders-empty">
                      {dataLoading
                        ? "Loading suppliers..."
                        : "No suppliers found."}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                )}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}


=== Vendors.jsx ===

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const API_URL = "http://localhost:5001/api";

const initialForm = {
  vendor_name: "",
  business_name: "",
  telephone: "",
  area: "",
  registration_date: "",
  status: "active",
};

export default function Vendors() {
  const [form, setForm] = useState(initialForm);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [vendors, setVendors] = useState([]);
  const [dataLoading, setDataLoading] = useState(true);
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null);
  const [deletingId, setDeletingId] = useState(null);

  async function loadVendors() {
    setError("");
    setDataLoading(true);
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/vendors`);
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.message || "Failed to load vendors.");
      }
      setVendors(Array.isArray(result) ? result : []);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message || "Could not load vendors.");
    } finally {
      setDataLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVendors();
  }, []);

  function handleChange(event) {
    const { name, value } = event.target;

    setForm((previousForm) => ({
      ...previousForm,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
    setLoading(true);

    try {
      const url = editingId
        ? `${API_URL}/vendors/${editingId}`
        : `${API_URL}/vendors`;
      const method = editingId ? "PUT" : "POST";

      const response = await fetch(url, {
        method,
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
        },
        body: JSON.stringify(form),
      });

      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.message || "Failed to register vendor.");
      }

      setMessage(
        result.message ||
          (editingId
            ? "Street vendor updated successfully!"
            : "Street vendor registered successfully!")
      );
      setForm({ ...initialForm });
      setEditingId(null);
      await loadVendors();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(
        err.message ||
          "Could not connect to the server. Check that the backend is running."
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function handleEdit(vendor) {
    setForm({
      vendor_name: vendor.vendor_name || "",
      business_name: vendor.business_name || "",
      telephone: vendor.telephone || "",
      area: vendor.area || "",
      registration_date: vendor.registration_date
        ? new Date(vendor.registration_date).toISOString().split("T")[0]
        : "",
      status: vendor.status || "active",
    });
    setEditingId(vendor.id);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  }

  async function handleDelete(id) {
    if (!confirm("Delete this vendor?")) return;
    setDeletingId(id);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`${API_URL}/vendors/${id}`, {
        method: "DELETE",
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(result.message || "Failed to delete vendor.");
      }
      setMessage(result.message || "Vendor deleted successfully.");
      await loadVendors();
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err.message);
    } finally {
      setDeletingId(null);
    }
  }

  function handleReset() {
    setForm({ ...initialForm });
    setEditingId(null);
    setMessage("");
    setError("");
  }

  return (
    <main className="vendor-page">
      <header className="vendor-heading">
        <h1>Street Vendors</h1>
        <p>Register a new street vendor in the system.</p>
      </header>

      <section className="vendor-form-card">
        <h2>Register Street Vendor</h2>
        <p className="form-description">
          Enter the vendor's details below.
        </p>

        {message && (
          <div className="vendor-success" role="status">
            {message}
          </div>
        )}

        {error && (
          <div className="vendor-error" role="alert">
            {error}
          </div>
        )}

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="vendor-form-grid">
            <div className="vendor-field">
              <label htmlFor="vendor_name">Vendor Name *</label>
              <input
                id="vendor_name"
                name="vendor_name"
                type="text"
                value={form.vendor_name}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter vendor's full name"
                required
                maxLength={100}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="vendor-field">
              <label htmlFor="business_name">Business Name</label>
              <input
                id="business_name"
                name="business_name"
                type="text"
                value={form.business_name}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter business name"
                maxLength={150}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="vendor-field">
              <label htmlFor="telephone">Telephone *</label>
              <input
                id="telephone"
                name="telephone"
                type="tel"
                value={form.telephone}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Enter telephone number"
                required
                maxLength={30}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="vendor-field">
              <label htmlFor="area">Trading Area *</label>
              <input
                id="area"
                name="area"
                type="text"
                value={form.area}
                onChange={handleChange}
                placeholder="Please enter trading area"
                required
                maxLength={150}
              />
            </div>

            <div className="vendor-field">
              <label htmlFor="registration_date">
                Registration Date *
              </label>
              <input
                id="registration_date"
                name="registration_date"
                type="date"
                value={form.registration_date}
                onChange={handleChange}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="vendor-field">
              <label htmlFor="status">Status</label>
              <select
                id="status"
                name="status"
                value={form.status}
                onChange={handleChange}
                required
              >
                <option value="active">Active</option>
                <option value="inactive">Inactive</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="vendor-form-actions">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading}>
              {loading ? "Saving..." : editingId ? "Update Vendor" : "Register Vendor"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              className="vendor-reset-button"
              onClick={handleReset}
            >
              {editingId ? "Cancel Edit" : "Clear Form"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="vendor-table-card">
        <h2>Registered Vendors</h2>
        {dataLoading ? (
          <p>Loading vendors...</p>
        ) : vendors.length === 0 ? (
          <p>No vendors registered yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="table-wrapper">
            <table className="vendor-table">
              <thead>
                <tr>
                  <th>ID</th>
                  <th>Vendor Name</th>
                  <th>Business Name</th>
                  <th>Telephone</th>
                  <th>Trading Area</th>
                  <th>Registration Date</th>
                  <th>Status</th>
                  <th>Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {vendors.map((vendor) => (
                  <tr key={vendor.id}>
                    <td>{vendor.id}</td>
                    <td>{vendor.vendor_name}</td>
                    <td>{vendor.business_name || "-"}</td>
                    <td>{vendor.telephone}</td>
                    <td>{vendor.area}</td>
                    <td>
                      {vendor.registration_date
                        ? new Date(vendor.registration_date).toISOString().split("T")[0]
                        : ""}
                    </td>
                    <td>{vendor.status}</td>
                    <td className="action-buttons">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="edit-btn"
                        onClick={() => handleEdit(vendor)}
                      >
                        Edit
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        className="delete-btn"
                        onClick={() => handleDelete(vendor.id)}
                        disabled={deletingId === vendor.id}
                      >
                        {deletingId === vendor.id ? "Deleting..." : "Delete"}
                      </button>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
